- On the dedicated server there is no wind tick and `updateFx` is `IsoPuddles` only; a thunder cloud meant to follow a random player (`ThunderStorm.startThunderCloud`) logs "Thundercloud couldnt target player..." and returns null when no player is connected, after the draws for the angle and the centre have been made (so storms of strength 0.7 or more start no cloud for their first cloud, tropical storms none; a second cloud and weaker storms run). A cloud that never ran keeps its start time, which the probe prints.
- `ClimateHistory` is a 12-line stub: `init` and `updateDayChange` do nothing.
- `Double.toString` and `Float.toString` (JDK 25): when the exact value lies halfway between two shortest candidates the even last digit is taken (2.35019683837890625 prints as `2.3501968383789062`); the shortest-digit routine of Rust rounded up, `java.rs` now takes the correctly rounded decimal of the shortest length when it reads back.
- HotSpot's `Math.cos`/`sin`/`tan`/`acos` intrinsics and the fdlibm port (`java_fdlibm.rs`) gave identical results at every value these scenarios reach (the thunder cloud tracks, the season day lengths, the climate curves); other inputs are not measured.

UNKNOWN / not reproduced:
- `ClimateMain.lua` (the only `OnClimateManagerInit` handler in the game's Lua): the handler is replayed by `climate_main_lua.rs`, which reads the player's `media/lua/server/Climate/ClimateMain.lua` at run time and replays exactly its statement forms (colour getters and `setExterior`/`setInterior`, `setSeasonColorDawn/Day/Dusk`), failing on anything else. It is not run on the Lua VM: ClimateManager, ClimateColorInfo and WeatherPeriod are not exposed to Lua yet. The Lua events `OnWeatherPeriodStart/Stage/Stop/Complete`, `OnInitModdedWeatherStage`, `OnUpdateModdedWeatherStage`, `OnClimateTick`, `OnThunderEvent` are not triggered.
- Network: `transmitClimatePacket`, the client's interpolation (wall clock) and `IsoPuddles` sync. Client and single-player parts: `windTick`, lightning application (`applyLightningForPlayer`), thunder sounds, thunder clouds that follow a player (players are not ported), `ClimateManager.updateFx`.
- Options not measured: `PerformanceSettings.fogQuality` other than 0 (the `== 2` branches are ported, untested), the weather shader and offscreen-buffer branches (`weatherShader`/`offscreen` 0 only), `Core.debug`, the sandbox Temperature option (the harness's `ErosionMain` season is the default 3), `Core.getOptionDisableLightningDuringStorms` (the default is assumed), `GameTime` rates other than 60 minutes per day with multiplier 1 and fps multiplier 2, the single-player sleeping multiplier, the time zone and daylight saving time of a player's machine (the port is UTC, as the probe pins), the harness world's meta grid bounds (-250..250 cells, taken from the probe's SETUP line; why that world reports them is UNKNOWN).
- The forecast `name` text (`Translator.getText("IGUI_Forecaster_Day")` plus the date) is not made: the date is kept in `name_date`.
- `climateHistory`, world flares and the climate-related statics of `Core` other than `forceSnow` are not modelled.

## The client's startup before the main menu, on the ported Lua VM (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh luagame` → `OK   luagame: 201101 lines identical`. `LuaGameProbe` runs the ORIGINAL client's steps in the original's order, headless, and then what `luaboot` runs (`LoadDirBase("shared")`, `LoadDirBase("client")`, `ZomboidGlobals.Load`, the boot events), and dumps what `luaboot` dumps plus the UI the scripts built (`UI toAdd|toRemove|UI`, `UICHILD`, `UITABLE`: every `UIElement` with its fields as raw bits and its Lua table, depth first). `pz-dump luagame` does the same on the port (`zombie::game_window::ClientStartup` before `LuaManager.init`, `exposed_game::init_shared` after it). The client is the one `-nosound` starts (`Core.soundDisabled`: the Dummy sound managers and bank, no FMOD). `luaload`, `luaserver`, `luaclient`, `luaboot`, `sandbox` and `translator` are unchanged (104755, 271055, 146715, 155934, 539, 119451 lines identical): their Lua state is the standalone one, only `luagame` has a client's startup behind it.

Steps of the original, in order (read from the decompiled `MainScreenState`, `GameWindow` and the game states; each run by the probe and ported unless listed under "cannot run"):
- `MainScreenState.main`: `RandStandard`/`RandLua.init`, `Bullet.init`, the `-nosound` argument, `InitDisplay`'s language option (no `options.ini` on a fresh cache: from `user.language`), the asset managers.
- `GameWindow.mainThreadInit`: `DebugOptions.init`, `GameProfiler.init`, the Dummy sound managers and `DummySoundBank`, `VoiceManager.loadConfig`, `ZomboidFileSystem.init`.
- `GameWindow.init`: `TextManager.Init` (fonts), the mods (`resetModFolders`, `resetDefaultModsForNewRelease`, `loadMods("default")`, `loadModPackFiles`), `Languages.init`, `Translator.language = null`, `initFonts`, `Translator.loadFiles`, `LuaManager.init`.
- `GameWindow.initShared`: the texture packs UI, UI2, IconsMoveables, RadioIcons, ApComUI, Mechanics, WeatherFx and their lookup, `IsoCursor`, `IsoReticle`, `PerkFactory.init` (42 `AddPerk` rows, the 47 perks, names translated), `CustomPerks`, `CustomSandboxOptions`, `ModRegistries`, `ScriptManager.Load` (items, trait and profession definitions, `Item.tags`), `SpriteModelManager`, `ClothingDecals`, `BeardStyles`, `HairStyles`, `OutfitManager`, `VoiceStyles`, then `RandStandard`/`RandLua.init` again, `Mouse.initCustomCursor`, the tile geometry, depth-texture assignment, seam and seating managers, `GameInput.initControllers`.
- `GameWindow.init` ends with `LoadDirBase` (shared, client), `ZomboidGlobals.Load`, `OnLoadSoundBanks`; `GameWindow.enter` (the tile texture packs of `texture2x`, `TileSeamManager`, `VoiceManager.InitVMClient`), `OnGameBoot`, `OnGameStateEnter` (a `TermsOfServiceState`) and `OnMainMenuEnter`.

Cannot run headless (each measured by running it in the probe):
- `ZomboidFileSystem.loadMods` needs `RandStandard.init` first (NullPointerException otherwise); `ScriptManager.Load` needs `Bullet.init` (the vehicle scripts call it); both are in the steps above.
- `Core.loadOptions` (`InitDisplay`) fails on the GLFW display modes (no display): left out; every option keeps its initialiser and the language comes from `user.language`.
- FMOD record devices (`Core.getOptionVoiceRecordDevice`, `VoiceManager.RecordDevices`) need a sound system: `-nosound` gives device 0 and an empty table.
- `Keyboard.initKeyNames` needs LWJGL's keyboard: not run, so `Keyboard.getKeyName` returns null for every code below 256 and `getKeyName(code)` (the global) returns null: this is where both runs stop (see below).
- `ModelManager.create` and `TileDepthTextureManager.init` abort the JVM without OpenGL (exit 134); `TileDepthMapManager.init` needs the latter's textures (NullPointerException): left out.
- `RandStandard`/`RandLua` take their seed from `SecureRandom` (`PZSeedGenerator`): the probe gives both a `CellularAutomatonRNG` with a fixed seed after `initShared`'s `init` (`{0,0,0,1}`, `{0,0,0,2}`), which the port uses too (nothing draws between).
- `Bullet.getPZBulletVersion` is a native library call: the measured value `"1.0.0.28"` is a constant in `zombie/core/core.rs`; `Core.getGitSha` is `"4a0e9546ec"` when `Core.IS_DEV` is false.

What the Lua does after `OnMainMenuEnter` (`MainScreen.lua:1686`): `MainScreen:instantiate` builds the main-menu UI and the options screen; in both runs it stops in `MainOptions:addKeybindingPanel` (`MainOptions.lua:2185`) with `__concat not defined for operands:  and null`, because `getKeyName(tonumber(v.key))` is null without `Keyboard.initKeyNames`. So the main menu does not open fully in either; the port reaches exactly the original's last state (the terms window in `UIManager.toAdd`/`toRemove`, the Lua globals and Java state the options screen filled).

Compared: the `luaboot` lines (order of the 1101 files loaded, every global and key added to an init table, every event handler, the Java state the scripts filled, the closures' upvalues, every `BOOT` line with the callback results), `STATIC <class>.<field>` lines for the static collections changed since the init snapshot, and the UI section. Ported for it: the client's startup (`zombie/game_window.rs`: `ClientStartup`, `Shared`; `PerkFactory.init`; `CharacterTraitDefinition`/`CharacterProfessionDefinition`; `ScriptManager` buckets, `Item.tags`; `CellularAutomatonRNG`), `UIElement` (full state and every method the Lua calls, `UIManager.AddUI/RemoveElement/clearArrays`, `UITransition`, `GameWindow.doRenderEvent`), `Core` (the 158 `newOption`/`newFakeOption` fields as ported `ConfigOption`s in `zombie/core/core_options.rs`, generated by `tools/gen_core_options.py` with the 241 one-line accessors; the colour options, key maps `addKeyBinding`/`getKey`/`reinitKeyMaps`, `getScreenModes`, `saveOptions` through `ConfigFile.write` into `<cache>/options.ini`, `getVersion`/`getGitSha`/`getBulletVersion`), `PerformanceSettings`, `SystemDisabler`, `MapGroups` (full: `createGroups`, `checkMapConflicts`, `addMissingVanillaDirectories`), `Keyboard.getKeyName`, `SandboxOptions.new`, `Translator.getAvailableLanguage`/`getLanguage` and `Language`, `zombie.core.Color`, the `VoiceManager` Lua table, `LuaManager`'s `cacheFileExists`/`getFileReader`/`getFileWriter` with `BufferedReader` and `LuaFileWriter`, `getLatestSave`/`getFullSaveDirectoryTable`, `DebugType.trace`. The exposure table gained `java.io.BufferedReader` and `LuaFileWriter` (`tools/gen_lua_expose.py`, same measurement). The files the port writes into the scratch cache dir equal the original's: `Lua/keysB42.ini`, `Lua/screenresolution.ini` (empty in the original's run, measured), `options.ini` (byte-identical, `cmp`), the `Saves` folder; the original also writes `Crafting/AllRecipes.txt`, `messaging/DebugOptions_list.xml` and `mods/*.txt`, which the port does not write (the Java steps that make them, the recipe, debug-option and mod-list code, are not part of the port's startup; the probe's comparison does not read them).

UNKNOWN:
- Whether the original's `getScreenModes` throws or only finds no mode without a display (either way the table is empty and the file empty).
- `LuaFileWriter` buffering: the port writes the text when the writer is closed; the original's encoder flushes earlier when its buffer fills, and loses a last buffer that is never closed. `BufferedReader` decodes the whole file at once (malformed UTF-8 becomes U+FFFD as in Rust, not checked against the JDK's ranges).
- `os.name` is taken from the build host (`macos` removes no option; others drop the two macOS-only options when writing `options.ini`): measured on macOS only.
- The methods the options screen would reach after the `getKeyName` stop (`setResolutionAndFullScreen`, `setOptionVSync`, the sound and voice setters, `setFramerate`, `zoomLevelsChanged`, ...) are not ported: their Java needs a window or a sound system.
- `HashSet` order of `JoypadManager.activeControllerGuids` (empty here).

## The game's boot events on the ported Lua VM (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh luaboot` → `OK   luaboot: 155934 lines identical`. What the original runs after loading its Lua on the way to the main menu, read from the decompiled `GameWindow` and game states: `GameWindow.init` ends with `LuaManager.LoadDirBase()` (shared, client), `ZomboidGlobals.Load()` and `triggerEvent("OnLoadSoundBanks")`; `GameWindow.enter` loads the texture packs and models, then `triggerEvent("OnGameBoot")`; then the state machine `initShared` filled enters `TISLogoState` (fires nothing), `TermsOfServiceState` (`triggerEvent("OnGameStateEnter", this)`, then `exit = true` unless Lua made its window) and `MainScreenState` (`triggerEvent("OnMainMenuEnter")`). The new probe `LuaBootProbe` (reusing `LuaLoadEnvProbe`'s load and report; `game_case` now compiles a probe with `-sourcepath tests/original_probe`) runs the original standalone: the client load (`shared client`), then those steps in that order; each event runs as `Event.trigger` does, every callback through `LuaManager.caller` on `LuaManager.thread`, by `pcall` so that each callback's result is printed (`BOOT <event> <n>`, then each callback `true`, or `false` with the error message). Measured ad hoc: the real `LuaEventManager.triggerEvent` / `TermsOfServiceState.enter()` path leaves a byte-identical report (all lines but the `BOOT` ones). `pz-dump luaboot` does the same on the port with its port of `Event.trigger` and `LuaEventManager.checkEvent`/`triggerEvent`. Compared after the events: everything `luaclient` compares (globals, added keys, event callbacks, Java state), then `ZomboidGlobals`' 41 static numbers (raw bits), `ObservationFactory.observationMap` (8 observations with their mutually exclusive lists), `SurvivorDesc`'s 11 trouser and 20 hair colours (raw float bits: `Color(r, g, b, a)` clamps to [0, 1], `ImmutableColor(r, g, b, a)` takes `Math.min(x, 1)`), `ScriptManager`'s `tagToItemMap` (the 10 tags `getItemsTag` was asked for, each list empty), the `TermsOfServiceState` flags, and a new upvalue section: the upvalues of every closure printed (1109 have any), then of the closures found in them, which is where the boot handlers keep their state (`ISMoveableDefinitions`' `instance` with its tool, material, scrap and repair definitions; `ISRadioInteractions`' instance; `DebugDemoTime`'s screen size 1280x720). The original's results standalone: `OnLoadSoundBanks` (1 callback, `SoundBanks.lua:368`) fails with `attempted index: addVoice of non-table: null` (`getFMODSoundBank()` is `BaseSoundBank.instance`, made by `GameWindow.mainThreadInit`, not run here); the 13 `OnGameBoot` callbacks all succeed (moveable definitions, traits, professions, observations, 3799 surnames, 1320 female and 993 male forenames as `SurvivorSurname_X`/`SurvivorName_X` because no translation is loaded, trouser and hair colours, radio interactions, the erosion debug screen size, the search-mode key bindings); `OnGameStateEnter` (1 callback, `ISTermsOfServiceUI.lua:174`) marks the state created (`fromLua0("created")`), finds terms version -1 (no `options.ini`) below 1, builds the window (`ISPanelJoypad` → `UIElement.new` and its setters) and fails in `createChildren` with `__add not defined for operands` (the large font height is nil, no font before `TextManager.Init`), so the state stays (`created` true, `exit` false); `OnMainMenuEnter` has no callback here (`MainScreen.lua` stops at line 12 while loading, `FONT_HGT_SMALL + 6` on a nil height, before it adds `LoadMainScreenPanel`). Ported for it (`zombie/lua/exposed_boot.rs` and their classes): `Event.trigger`, `LuaEventManager.checkEvent`/`triggerEvent`, `zombie.ZomboidGlobals.Load`, `zombie.gameStates.TermsOfServiceState` (`fromLua0`, the end of `enter`), `zombie.characters.traits.ObservationFactory` (`addObservation`, `setMutualExclusive`), `SurvivorFactory.addSurname`/`addFemaleForename`/`addMaleForename` (`Translator.getText` of the prefixed name), `SurvivorDesc.addTrouserColor`/`addHairColor`, `ColorInfo.toColor`/`toImmutableColor`, `Color(float, float, float, float)`, `ImmutableColor(float, float, float, float)`, `PerkFactory.getPerkName` (a perk's name is its id until `PerkFactory.init`), `ScriptManager.getItemsTag` (the per-tag cache), `CharacterTraitDefinition.getTraits` and `CharacterProfessionDefinition.getProfessions` (new empty lists: the definitions come from `ScriptManager.Load`), `zombie.ui.UIElement` (constructors, `setX`/`setY`/`setWidth`/`setHeight`, the four anchors, `setWantKeyEvents`, `setWantExtraMouseEvents`, `setForceCursorVisible`, `setConsumeMouseEvents`), `Core.getScreenWidth`/`getScreenHeight` (`Core.width`/`height`, 1280x720) and `getTermsOfServiceVersion` (-1), the globals `getFMODSoundBank` (nil), `getScriptManager`, `instanceof` and `getISUIStackTrace` (nil without `Core.debug`). The exposure grows to 99 classes (3360 method names): the above plus `TermsOfServiceState` and `UIElement`. `luaload` (104755), `luaserver` (271055) and `luaclient` (146715) stay identical. Not reproduced (this state, the original's as well): `GameWindow.initShared` before `LoadDirBase` (`PerkFactory.init`, `ScriptManager.Load`, the trait and profession scripts, `Translator.loadFiles`), so in the real game the perk names, trait/profession descriptions, item lists and names differ from this standalone state; the sound bank (`mainThreadInit`), textures and models (`enter`); the events of every frame from the first one (`OnRenderTick` in `JoyPadSetup.lua`, `OnPostUIDraw` in `TutorialSetup.lua`, `MainScreenState.update`'s `OnFETick`): the frame loop is not ported; the terms window past `createChildren` and the main menu (`LoadMainScreenPanel`), which need fonts after `TextManager.Init` and `UIManager`. UNKNOWN: `instanceof` with a superclass or interface name (the port's classes carry no hierarchy; it answers false); `UIElement.toString` (`tostring` of its table) and `HashMap.toString` of `observationMap`; the real game's boot with an `options.ini` that accepted the terms (version 1: the window is skipped and `exit` is set).

## XUI scripts (UI skins, styles, layouts) by direct port (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh xui` → `OK   xui: 98089 lines identical`. The booted original server (`XuiProbe` in `ServerHarness`) against the port (`pz-dump xui`). Compared, every instance field by reflection in declaration order (hash maps by sorted key, floats/doubles with `Float`/`Double.toString`, colours as rgba): the six XUI script lists of `ScriptManager` sorted by full type (`getAllXuiConfigScripts`: 1, `project_zomboid` with its 8 var-type key lists; `getAllXuiLayouts`, `getAllXuiStyles`, `getAllXuiDefaultStyles`, `getAllXuiColors`: 0, vanilla has no `xuiLayout`, `xuiStyle`, `xuiDefaultStyle` or `xuiGlobalColors` block, only comments mention them; `getAllXuiSkinScripts`: 4) with module, enabled/debugOnly, `scriptVersion` (`PZHash.murmur_64`) and body count; per skin script its imports, default and 238 aliased entity UI scripts (`displayName` through `Translator.getRecipeName` with the spaces removed, components by `ComponentType.valueOf`, `listOrderZ`/`enabled` with their set flags), the style blocks of 50 Lua classes (unnamed and named blocks, surrounding quotes trimmed, empty/`nil`/`null` stored as null) and its `colors` block (`r:g:b:a` and `rgb:` forms). Then the static state `XuiManager.ParseScripts` leaves: `XuiLuaStyle.ALLOWED_VAR_TYPES` and the var registry (229 keys from the config, each an `XuiVar` subclass with its default: Boolean false, Double 0.0, FontType `UIFont.Small`, the others null), every static field of `XuiManager` (`parseOnce`/`hasParsedOnce` false after `loadScripts`' `setParseOnce(false)`, the layout/style maps and lists empty), and the 4 `XuiSkin`s (`default`, `electric_common`, `fluid_separator`, `skin_furnace_processor`; the last three import `default`): imports, 36 colours, the default and 238 entity UI styles (a new one starts as a copy of the default; icons stay null, `Texture.trygetTexture` returns null on a server), and per Lua class the default style and named styles (77 in `default`) with every var in list order: `vars` follows the `HashMap` order of each block's keys (`java::hash_map_order`), a named style starts with a copy of the default style's vars, colours resolve through the skin's map, then `Colors.GetColorByName` (`Lime`, `Crimson`), then the `r : g : b : a` form; font names drop a `UIFont.` prefix. Last, the 144 names of `zombie.core.Colors` in order (2 game, 142 standard; no name repeats, in any case) with `GetColorByName` (lower-cased lookup) and `GetColorInfo`'s set, and the 25 colour-blind ones with their values.

Ported: `zombie.scripting.objects.XuiConfigScript`, `XuiColorsScript`, `XuiLayoutScript` (`Load` keeps the text), `XuiSkinScript` (with `EntityUiScript`, `ComponentUiScript`, `StyleInfoScript`), a shared `BaseScriptObject` (`InitLoadPP`, `LoadCommonBlock`, `calculateScriptVersion`), `zombie.scripting.ui.XuiManager` (`ParseScripts`, `GetSkin`, `GetDefaultSkin`, `GetLayoutScript`, `GetStyleScript`, `GetDefaultStyleScript`, `setParseOnce`, `reset`), `XuiSkin` (`Load` with imports, `LoadColors`, the default/named style and entity loaders, `color`, `get`, `getDefault`, `getEntityUiStyle`, `getComponentUiStyle`, `EntityUiStyle`/`ComponentUiStyle`/`StyleInfo`), `XuiLuaStyle` (`ReadConfigs`, `loadVar`, `copyVarsFrom`, `addVar`, every `XuiVar` subclass's `fromString`/`copy`/`getValueString`), `XuiVarType`, `XuiAutoApply`, `XuiScriptType`, `zombie.core.Colors` (the static tables, `AddGameColor`, the lookups) and `zombie.entity.ComponentType` (names and ids). In `ScriptManager.load` the six XUI buckets load after the timed actions (`ScriptBucket.LoadScripts`: ResetExisting for all but xuiSkin, whose 251 bodies of `default` amend one script), then `ParseScripts` runs once in PostLoadScripts. Re-run after the change: `items` (5092 lines), `recipes` (33693), `vehicles` (354986), `scriptparser` (196) all OK, goldens unchanged.

Lua-facing API the client scripts call (for the Lua wiring): `XuiManager.GetDefaultSkin` (ISXuiSkin, ISEntityUI, ISEntityBuildMenu), `XuiManager.GetSkin`, `XuiManager.GetLayout` (ISXuiBuilder), `XuiManager.GetLayoutScript`, `GetStyleScript`, `GetCombinedScripts` (XuiDebugWindow); on a skin `get(luaClass, style)`, `getDefault(luaClass)`, `color(name)`, `getEntityUiStyle`, `getEntityDisplayName`, `getComponentUiStyle` and `instanceof(skin, "XuiSkin")`; on a style `getVars`, `getVar`, `getXuiLuaClass`; on a var `getLuaTableKey`, `getType`, `isValueSet`, `value`, `getValueString`, `getAutoApplyMode`, `getR`/`getG`/`getB`/`getA`, `getTexture`; on entity/component UI styles `getDisplayName`, `getIcon`, `getLuaPanelClass`, `getListOrderZ`, `getXuiStyle`, `getLuaWindowClass`, `getBuildDescription`, `getDescription`, `getLuaOpenWindow`, `getLuaCanOpenWindow`, `isEnabled`; the enums `XuiAutoApply` (No, IfSet, IfSetAndKeyExists, Forbidden) and `XuiVarType` (Color, Function, StringList, Texture, Vector). None of it is exposed to the ported Lua VM yet.

UNKNOWN / not ported: `XuiScript`, `XuiTableScript`, `XuiReference`, `VectorPosAlign`, `TextAlign` (the layout side: `XuiLayoutScript.preParse` reports "not ported" and nothing is registered; no vanilla data to measure it with). Not exercised by vanilla, so unmeasured: `XuiStringList.fromString` (no skin sets a StringList key), a var key the registry lacks (the exception that ends `ParseScripts`; the original's `loadScripts` then skips the remaining PostLoadScripts and every OnScriptsLoaded, which the port does not model), an unknown import, a colour or number that does not parse, an unknown `ComponentType`, `xuiGlobalColors` (`Colors.AddGameColor`), reload (`ResetOnceOnReload`, `parseOnce`). The order of the enum-keyed maps (`varConfigs`, `componentUi*Map`; identity hash in the original) is not reproduced and is not observable in what is compared. `Translator.getText` of the `TranslateString` vars and display names (the Lua getters), textures on a client, and `XuiSkin.debugPrint` are not ported.

## Vehicle scripts by direct port (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh vehicles` → `OK   vehicles: 354986 lines identical`. The booted original server (`VehiclesProbe` in `ServerHarness`) against the port (`pz-dump vehicles`): all 241 `vehicle` scripts of `media/scripts` (`ScriptManager.getAllVehicleScripts`; all in module Base, none dropped) sorted by full name, then all 69 `template vehicle` blocks (`getAllVehicleTemplates`) sorted by full type, with name, body length and `String.hashCode`, and, for the 44 templates whose `getScript()` a vehicle or another template asked for, the VehicleScript it built (the other 25 are only used through `template!`, which reads the body and never builds the script). Every instance field of VehicleScript and of its nested objects is printed by reflection in declaration order (floats with `Float.toString`, hash maps and sets sorted, Lua tables by key with `n:`/`s:` key types): module, enabled/debugOnly, `scriptVersion` (`PZHash.murmur_64` of the full type, mod ids and bodies), loaded body count, `fileName` (the file of the last body), name, models, attachments (475, `ModelAttachment` with its owner), mass and the other physics floats, extents/shadow/centre of mass, physics shapes (box/sphere/mesh validation), wheels and crawl-through wheels (`getWheelById` only looks in `wheels`), passengers (380: anims, switch seats with their seat index, positions), the engine/health ints, forced colour, siren colours (`ImmutableColor` clamped, 27 vehicles), RPM type, `crawlOffsets` (all 241: `initCrawlOffsets` with its 0.15 thinning in float order), zombie types, the special key ring (62 vehicles, each entry through `ScriptManager.resolveItemType`), gear ratios, textures and skins (326; the Texture objects stay null on a server), areas, parts (7992: item types suffixed with the mechanic type when `specificItem`, containers (2106) with their seat index and sound map, Lua tables built by `LoadTable`/`checkIntegerKey` and deep-copied by `LuaManager.copyTable`, Lua functions, models, door, window, anims), the lightbar and the sounds (`specified`, the horn copied into the alarm when there is no alarm). `Loaded` runs as on a dedicated server: every size and offset times the first model's scale, `toBullet` skipped (`GameServer.server`). Templates: `template = Name` copies areas, parts, passengers, the specified sounds (not alarm or horn), wheels and physics (cleared first, with `useChassisPhysicsCollision`) from the template's lazily built script; `template = Name/part/Id` (and area, passenger, wheel, physics) copies one; `template! = Name` loads the template's body into the vehicle itself; `part Door* { ... }` and `passenger * { ... }` amend every existing part/passenger the glob matches. Ported for it: `zombie/scripting/objects/vehicle_script.rs` (`VehicleScript.Load`, `LoadTemplate`, `copyAreasFrom`/`copyPartsFrom`/`copyPassengersFrom`/`copySoundFrom`/`copyWheelsFrom`/`copyPhysicsFrom`, every `Load*` of a child block, `LoadTable`, `checkIntegerKey`, `globMatch`, `Loaded`, `initCrawlOffsets`, `isOverlappingWheel`, the nested classes with their `makeCopy` quirks: `Position.makeCopy` drops `area`, `PhysicsShape.makeCopy` drops `physicsShapeScript`), `vehicle_template.rs` (`VehicleTemplate.getScript`), `model_attachment.rs`, `org/joml/vector2f.rs`, `ImmutableColor(float, float, float)`, `KahluaTable` rawset-nil/rawget, and in `script_manager.rs` the VehicleTemplate bucket (`ScriptBucket.Template.CreateFromTokenPP`: a later template of the same name replaces the earlier), the vehicle buckets (`InitLoadPP` per body, every body loaded into the same script since the Vehicle type has no ResetExisting flag, RemoveLoadError, `onScriptLoad` → `Loaded` before `scriptMap.put`, the HashMap order of `getAllScripts`), `getVehicleTemplate` and `resolveItemType`. Not reproduced: a template reached again through another template while it is still being built gets the original's partly loaded script (UNKNOWN here; nothing is copied, logged; no game template does it); `globMatch` patterns with regex metacharacters other than `*` and `.` (UNKNOWN; none in the game); a `template!` loop ends after 64 levels with an error where the original overflows the stack; the order of `getAllVehicleTemplates` (the probe sorts). The exception paths (a value without `=`, a bad number, a null area/anim/position/physics id, a null attachment id, a value without `=` in a `lua` block, a null wheel id in `Loaded`) are ported literally but not exercised by the game's scripts.

ScriptParser fix found on the way (reported by the fonts work, measured here): in the original `ScriptParser.readBlock` the for-loop's `i++` still runs after a child block returns, so the character right after a child's `}` is never examined (a `,`, `{` or `}` there stays in the text of the next value or header), and a block header is split with `trim().split("\\s+")`, Java's whitespace only (a no-break space or em space is part of the type or id). The port examined that character and split on Unicode whitespace. `tests/script_parser_cases.txt` with `ScriptParserProbe` (the original `ScriptParser.stripComments`, `parseTokens` and `parse` over crafted text): `sh tools/oracle.sh scriptparser` → `OK   scriptparser: 196 lines identical` after the fix (before: `a { x = 1, }, b = 2,` gave the original the value `", b = 2"` where the port had an empty value and `" b = 2"`; `}}`, `}{` and no-break-space headers differed too). No game script has a `}` followed directly by `,`, `{` or `}`, or a header with non-ASCII whitespace, so no game output changes; the cases that go through ScriptParser (ScriptManager.load, `IsoWorld.init`, `fonts_file`) were re-run after the fix, all identical: `items` 5092, `recipes` 33693, `vehicles` 354986, `fonts` 1023561, `entitysprites` 879, `collide` 1315, `specialcollide` 5112, `doortoggle` (5112), `stairs` 73, `los` 64, `losdoors` (both runs, hashes unchanged), `chunksave` 90883 lines. `mover`/`movercollide` (the zomboid-scene examples, which also boot `IsoWorld.init`) were not re-run here.

## The game's own `media/lua/client` on the ported Lua VM (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh luaclient` → `OK   luaclient: 146715 lines identical`. The original `LuaManager.init`, then `LoadDirBase("shared")` and `LoadDirBase("client")`, standalone (`LuaLoadEnvProbe` with `shared client`), against the port (`pz-dump luaclient`). Compared as for `luaserver` (below): the order of the 1101 files loaded (374 shared, 727 client), every global and every key added to an init table with its whole value (140017 global lines and 5205 added-key lines: the ISUI class tables, `Joypad`/`JoypadState` with their tables keyed by `JoypadButton`/`JoypadAxis*` constants, the `gamepadBinding` presets, PZAPI's atom UI classes that keep `AtomUI*` statics tables, ...), the callbacks of the 80 events that have any, all 268 event names (the client adds 6 with `LuaEventManager.AddEvent`), and the Java state. The Java state section now also prints the client's registries, on all three cases: `MusicIntensityConfig` (the 24 events of `Music/MusicIntensityConfig.lua` with intensity bits, duration and `multiple`, then the id map in key order) and `MusicThreatConfig` (the 7 statuses with intensity and override), both `nil` until `getInstance()`; the binding of every `CharacterJoypadButtonBinding` and `CharacterJoypadAxis2dBinding` constant (`gamepadBinding.lua` applies the "Original" preset while loading: `CharacterInputBindingSet.resetAllToDefault`, then `Run`, `Sprint` and `PrecisionAim` rebound through three different `setBinding` overloads); `CharacterInputBindingSet.loadedBindingSets` (`[]`: no preset file in the fresh cache dir; `nil` in the other two cases). `luaload` (104755 lines) and `luaserver` (271055 lines) stay identical with these 33 new lines. The original itself has 218 Lua errors while loading the client (mostly `FONT_HGT_* + n` on a nil font height: `TextManager.getFontHeight` throws a NullPointerException before the renderer's `TextManager.Init`, which `MethodCaller` swallows, 442 times); the port raises the same 218 messages and logs the same 442 swallowed exceptions (compared from the two logs, outside the oracle). Measured with `LuaLoadCallsProbe` (`shared client`): 78 distinct Java functions are called while loading. Ported for it: `zombie.audio.MusicIntensityConfig` (`getInstance`, `initEvents`) and `MusicThreatConfig` (`initStatuses`, the status getters and override), `zombie.characters.CharacterJoypadButtonBinding` (the 28 constants' default bindings, `setBinding` with MultiLuaJavaInvoker's overload choice, `setDefault`, `setAllToDefault`, the getters), `CharacterJoypadAxis2dBinding`, `CharacterInputBindingSet` (`getLoadedBindingSets`, `reloadAll` over `<cachedir>/InputBindings`, `resetAllToDefault`), `zombie.input.ControllerButtonStyle`, `Core.getOptionFontSizeReal` (option 6 from `Core.height` 720: 1), `getOptionControllerButtonStyleString` ("XBOX") and `getOptionGamepadBindingPreset` ("Original"), `TextManager.getCurrentCodeFont` (`UIFont.CodeSmall`) and `getFontFromEnum` (nil: no font before Init), `org.joml.Vector3f` (`new()` and `toString`, read from the jar's bytecode with `javap` as JOML is not decompiled; checked against 10 strings the original printed, with signed zeros, rounding ties, subnormals, NaN and infinities), `KahluaTableImpl.rawgetFloat`, `StringUtils.discardNullOrWhitespace`, the globals `getSteamModeActive`, `isDemo`, `getCellSizeInSquares`, `getChunkSizeInSquares`, `getMaxActivePlayers`, `getButtonCount` and `isJoypadConnected` (the 16 controller slots of `GameWindow.GameInput` are empty before GLFW is polled), `CGlobalObjects.getSystemCount`/`getSystemByIndex`/`getSystemByName`, `CloseWindowState`/`OpenWindowState.instance`, `DebugType:debugln`/`warn` (into the port's log). The exposure grows to 90 classes (3019 method names; `tools/gen_lua_expose.py`): the above plus `JoypadButton`, `JoypadAxis1d`, `JoypadAxis2d` and the four `AtomUI` classes. Measured on the way: `LuaJavaInvoker.prepareCall` accepts one argument for a static method without parameters (a `Class:method()` call such as `CharacterInputBindingSet:getLoadedBindingSets()`), and otherwise wants the exact count; the port's client functions check the same. Not reproduced: reading a binding preset file (`PZXmlUtil.parse`, JAXB: a preset in `<cachedir>/InputBindings` is a "not ported" error), the other 11 `Vector3f` constructors and its methods, `setBinding` with arguments no overload takes, `rawgetFloat` of a boxed `java.lang.Float`, `Texture.nullTextures` (still left out). UNKNOWN: the order MultiLuaJavaInvoker tries overloads with the same parameter count (the `getMethods()` order, not stable between runs), which decides ambiguous calls such as `setBinding(nil, x)`; a connected controller; mods; the debug log text (the original's `DebugLog` prints nothing in this state; the port keeps its own log); the `MapSymbolDefinitions` registry the shared scripts fill (not in the Java state print).

## Fonts (.fnt) by direct port (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh fonts` → `OK   fonts: 1023561 lines identical`. The original `zombie.core.fonts.AngelCodeFont` and `zombie.ui.TextManager` run standalone (`FontsProbe`, a `game_case`: no renderer, so every texture lookup fails, as it does headless) against the port (`pz-dump fonts`). Compared: all 198 `.fnt` files (185 under `media/fonts`, 13 in `media/`; none fails) loaded the way `TextManager` loads one (`new AngelCodeFont("media/...fnt", null)`): the resolved file, every defined character (895,981) in id order with all its fields, the sorted kerning arrays of the 14,554 characters that have kerning (334,956 kerning lines, 4,373 of them repeated pairs: the first pair's amount wins), the derived line height, the length of `chars`, the 1,988 page texture names `parseFnt` asks `Texture.getSharedTexture` for (recorded through the shared `Bucket`'s map, the last table that lookup asks; `Texture.nullTextures` and the shared table are cleared before each font), and `getWidth` (with and without `xadvance`, and on a sub-range), `getHeight` (lines, actual height, offset) and `getYOffset` on 19 sample strings: ASCII, multi-line, empty, a lone newline, CRLF and lone CR, tab and NUL, Latin-1, Polish, Cyrillic, Chinese, Japanese, Korean, Thai, characters no font has (U+0001, U+007F, U+FFFD, U+FFFF, an emoji surrogate pair), rich-text markup (plain characters to this class) and a long sentence. Then `Core.getOptionFontSizeReal` for options 1..6 and 29 screen heights; the 29 languages (`Languages.init`); the 9 `fonts*.txt` files through `FontsFile.read` (every entry); `TextManager.getFontFilePath` (private, by reflection) for every language, size folder and named file (10,585 lookups); and `TextManager.Init` on a fresh instance for every language × font size option 1..5 × dyslexic flag (290 runs): the font of every `UIFont` (which ones share one object), `normal[]`, the 19 font fields, `isUsingNonEnglishFonts`, `getFontHeight`/`MeasureFont`/`isSdf` for every `UIFont` and null, `MeasureStringX/Y/YReal/YOffset` and `CentreStringYOffset` on 6 samples, and `WrapText` on 17 cases for 5 fonts (words cut into hyphenated pieces when too wide for an empty line, Chinese text in the CJK fonts, `maxLines` with a suffix appended or replacing the line's end, `\r?\n` splitting with trailing empty lines dropped, an empty word at a negative width throwing StringIndexOutOfBoundsException), `getAllFonts`, `UIFont.FromString`. Measured on the way: the `info` and `common` lines are read and dropped, so the file's `lineHeight`, `base`, `padding`, `spacing` and `scaleW/H` are never used; the line height is the tallest `height + yoffset` of all characters but the space, which differs from the file's `common lineHeight` in 33 of the 198 files, both ways (all 15 `code*.fnt`, e.g. EN 1x `codeLarge` 30 instead of 33; the 10 SDF fonts, e.g. `sdfRegular` 48 instead of 45 and `sdfRobertoSans` 76 instead of 69; three TH fonts; `mainfont`, `mainfont2`, `zombiefont3a/4/5`). `fonts.txt` lines are joined without separators before `ScriptParser` parses them. Font size option 1 uses `media/fonts/<file>`, options 2..5 the `1x`..`4x` folders; option 6 ("auto") maps screen heights below 1080 to 1, 1080-1211 to 2, 1212-1475 to 3, 1476-1871 to 4, from 1872 to 5. A language without its own folder (FR, DE, ... 21 of 29) gets EN's table and files; `mainfont`, `mainfont2`, `zombiefont5` and the credits fonts resolve to `media/` itself (TH has its own credits fonts); MediumNew and the AutoNorm fonts are null and measure with Small; DebugConsole shares Small; in the dyslexic table the Sdf fonts have no `type = sdf`, so `isSdf` is false; `getYOffset` throws ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException for a character past a font's last id, where `getWidth`/`getHeight` skip it. Ported: `zombie/core/fonts/angel_code_font.rs` (`AngelCodeFont` constructor from a file name, `parseFnt`, `parseChar`, `CharDef.getKerning`, `getWidth` ×4, `getHeight` ×2, `getLineHeight`, `getYOffset`, `isSdf`/`setSdf`, `destroy`), `zombie/ui/{ui_font, fonts_file, fonts_file_font, text_manager}.rs` (`UIFont`, `FontsFile.read`/`fromString`, `TextManager.Init`, `getFontFilePath`, `getFontFromEnum`, `getFontHeight`, `isSdf`, `getAllFonts`, `getNormalFromFontSize`, `isUsingNonEnglishFonts`, `MeasureStringX/Y/YReal/YOffset`, `CentreStringYOffset`, `MeasureFont`, `WrapText` ×2, `getCurrentCodeFont`), `zombie/core/core.rs` (`Core.getOptionFontSizeReal`), `zombie/zomboid_file_system.rs` (`init`'s file map over `media`, `getRelativeFile`, `getString`), `ScriptParser.Block.getValue`, and in `java.rs` `Short.parseShort`, `BufferedReader.readLine`'s line splitting, `StringTokenizer`, `compareToIgnoreCase`, `File` path normalization and `getParent`. Not reproduced (renderer): texture loading (`fontImage`, the page textures: the port keeps the names asked for; `pages` stays empty in both), `CharDef.init`/`draw`/`image`, `CharDefTexture`, `drawString`/`render`, the display-list caches (`displayListCaching` is never set in the original either), `onStateChanged`, `isLoading`/`isAnyFontLoading`, the SDF shader `Init` creates last, the `DrawString*`/`TextDrawObject`/BBcode methods, the constructor `AngelCodeFont(String, Texture)`. UNKNOWN: mods' fonts and `fonts.txt`; malformed UTF-8 in a `.fnt` (the port decodes with the JDK's `String` rules, the original reads through `InputStreamReader`; the game's files are ASCII); a read error after the file opened (the original logs it and keeps `chars` null; the port returns an error); exception messages (only the class names are compared); which bad kerning entry fails first (the original walks a Trove hash map); `"."`/`".."` segments and percent-encoding in `getRelativeFile`; a `language.json` the original rejects (the port takes every language folder that has one); option 6 under a real screen height; the fonts on a real renderer.

## Translations by direct port (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh translator` → `OK   translator: 119451 lines identical`. `TranslatorProbe` runs the original `zombie.core.Translator` standalone (`game_case`: the pinned jar from the game root, no window, no server; `ZomboidFileSystem.init` on a fresh cache dir, then `Languages.init`; its stderr reads `ORACLE Core.IS_DEV=false Core.debug=false Translator.debug=false mods=[] perks=0`, so `initTranslations` has no perk to name). `pz-dump translator` runs the port (`zombie/core/translator.rs`, `languages.rs`, `language.rs`, `language_file.rs`, `org/json/`, `java_formatter.rs`) on the same command file, `tests/translator_cases.txt` (433 lines). The same probe run by hand on the game's own JRE (Zulu 25.0.5, `--release 25`) printed the same bytes as on the JDK 27 that `oracle.sh` uses (sha256 `d0635984…`, the golden).

Compared:
- `Languages`: the 29 languages of `media/lua/shared/Translate` (name, display text, base, azerty; printed sorted by name, see UNKNOWN) and the default, EN.
- 35 loads (`Core.setOptionLanguageName`, then `Translator.loadFiles`): each of the 29 languages once (EN, RU, then the others by name), then `es_mx` (other letter case), the unknown `xx` and the `_` and blank options, which fall back to `user.language` (set by the probe to `en`, `pl` or the unknown `zz`, which falls back to EN), and EN again. For each load: the language `getLanguage()` resolved, its fallback stack, each of the 30 tables (`Tooltip` … `Credits`) and `Recorded_Media` in EN, in full for EN and RU and as an FNV-1a-64 digest of the sorted entries for the others; the `missing` set and the `<br>` cache. `loadFiles` keeps the `entity` table, `Recorded_Media` in EN and the `<br>` cache from earlier loads (the original's own quirk), and the port does the same.
- After every load, 148 lookups: 90 `getText` keys (every prefix branch, too few and too many arguments, `<br>`, `%%`, empty values, missing keys), `getTextOrNull`, `getDisplayItemName`, `getItemNameFromFullType`, `setDefaultItemEvolvedRecipeName`/`getItemEvolvedRecipeName`, `getMoveableDisplayName`/`getMoveableDisplayNameOrNull`, `getRecipeName`, `getRecipeGroupName`, `getTextMediaEN`, `getAttributeText`/`getAttributeTextOrNull`, `getFluidText`, `getEntityText` and `getMapLabelText`.
- 163 format templates put in the UI table and read through `getText` and `getTextOrNull` with string, null, boolean, double and plain-object arguments: `java.util.Formatter` through `String.formatted`, then the original's fallback for malformed templates (`MissingFormatArgumentException` gives the unformatted text; another `IllegalFormatException` retries after the two regex rewrites, then gives the text). They include the 19 templates of the 2026-11-10 `TranslatorProbe` (below), whose file this probe replaces.
- 76 JSON texts read through the private `tryFillMapFromFile` (org.json 20250517, not strict), each into the same table in order: 33 give a table, 43 an exception (28 `JSONException` with its message and position, 11 `ClassCastException` for values that are not strings, 2 `MalformedInputException` for invalid UTF-8, 2 `NullPointerException` for `null` values). These texts cover unquoted keys and values, number and literal keys (`1E+2`, `1E-7`, `-0.0`, `1.50`, `1.5f` as `1.5`, `null`, `true`), keys that collide only after that conversion (`-0`/`-0.0` and `true`/`TRUE` are duplicate-key errors), escapes, a BOM, NUL, trailing text, nested arrays and objects, and an empty value that does not replace an earlier one.

Ported: `Translator` (`loadFiles`, `tryFillMapFromFile`, `formatFixer`, `getText`/`getTextOrNull` with `getTextInternal`, `fixupArgs` and `reportMissingArgumentsFromPastAbuse`, `setLanguage`, `getLanguage`, `getAvailableLanguage`, `getDefaultLanguage`, `getAzertyMap`, `getUI` and the lookups above); `Languages` (`init`, `loadTranslateDirectory`, `getByName`, `getIndexByName`, `getByIndex`), `Language`, `LanguageFile`; org.json `JSONTokener`, `JSONObject` (parsing, `opt*`, `stringToValue`, `stringToNumber`, `BigDecimal`/`Double` text) and `JSONArray` parsing; `java.util.Formatter` (parser, specifier checks, argument indexing, the general conversions `b B h H s S`, `%%`, `%n`, and every conversion of a null or wrong-class argument). `ScriptManager` now takes item display names from `Translator::start(root, "EN")`; the `items`, `chunksave` and `tiledefs` golden hashes still match.

Not reproduced: Lua does not see this translator yet (`zombie/lua/exposed_methods.rs` still treats every key as missing; `zomboid-lua::text` below is unchanged); mods (`Languages` and `Translator` read the game's folder only), `readMapTranslation`/`readModTranslation` (`tempMap`), `getMoveableDisplayName(IsoObject)`, and the `Core.IS_DEV`/`Core.debug`/`Translator.debug` branches (all false in the probe). `java.util.Formatter` numeric, character and date conversions of an argument of their own class (an Integer to `%d`, a Double to `%f`, `%c`, `%t`) give `NotPorted` (PLACEHOLDER). Lua passes numbers as Doubles, so `%d` of a Lua number takes the ported `IllegalFormatConversionException` path. The game's files hold only `%N$s` after `formatFixer` and one `%02d` (CS `IGUI_CraftUI_TimeRequiredFormat`). `user.language` (the JVM's, from the OS locale) is `en` in the port (PLACEHOLDER); the probe sets it.

UNKNOWN:
- The order of the `Languages` list: the directory stream's, as in Java, and compared sorted.
- Which exception the original reports when one JSON text has two bad values: it walks `toMap()`'s `HashMap` order, the port the file's.
- A lone surrogate from a `\u` escape: Java keeps it; the port reads U+FFFD.
- Non-ASCII digits in `\u` escapes and numbers: Java accepts them.
- Hexadecimal floats as unquoted values or keys (`0x1.8p1`): `Double.valueOf` reads them; the port keeps a string.
- Formatter behaviour beyond the cases.

None of these occurs in the game's 29 languages: no BOM, duplicate key, value that is not a string, or `\u` escape.

## Crafting recipes (craftRecipe scripts) by direct port (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh recipes` → `OK   recipes: 33693 lines identical`. The booted original server (`RecipesProbe` in `ServerHarness`) against the port (`pz-dump recipes`): all 969 `craftRecipe` scripts of `media/scripts` (`ScriptManager.getAllCraftRecipes`; all in module Base, none dropped), sorted by full type, with the fields `CraftRecipe.Load`, `InitLoadPP`, `OnScriptsLoaded` and `OnPostWorldDictionaryInit` set, read by reflection: name, translation name (`Translator.getRecipeName`, EN `Recipes.json`), enabled/debugOnly, mod id, `scriptVersion` (`PZHash.murmur_64` of the full type, mod ids and bodies), icon name (with its `Item_` prefix), recipe group, needToBeLearn, SkillRequired/AutoLearnAny/AutoLearnAll/xpAward, MetaRecipe, time, the timed action (its name and the TimedActionScript `getTimedActionScript` finds, 889 recipes), the five Lua calls, OnAddToMenu, category, tags, Animation, Tooltip, AllowBatchCraft, CanWalk, `researchSkillLevel` (342 recipes end with a value other than -1, two of them from a `ResearchSkillLevel` key; the rest are written during OnPostWorldDictionaryInit: `OutputMapper.getItem` calls `canBeResearched()`, whose `getResearchSkillLevel` computes and normalises it), ResearchAll/Any; the 4398 io lines in `ioLines` order (3410 inputs including 55 `-` lines, 988 outputs) with type, original line, amount/maxamount, amounts/maxamounts (the "mixed amounts" rule that forces 1), loaded items/fluids/energies, item tags (`ItemTag.get`; printed sorted, as the original's `HashSet` iterates by identity hash), fluid categories, accepts-any flags, item apply mode (with the Keep a `-` line forces in OnPostWorldDictionaryInit), fluid match mode, applyOnTick, shapedIndex, flags (EnumSet order), the `+`/`-`/parent links, chance; every output mapper (225 named ones from `itemMapper` blocks and `mappers[...]`, plus the own mapper of each plain item output) with its default and entrees, the items they resolve to (`resultItems`: a shared mapper adds its items once per output that resolves it), `entreeMap` and registered inputs; prop1/prop2, tool left/right, usesTools, hasOnTick; the overlay mapper (37 recipes) with default styles, item styles and input. Ported for it: `zombie/scripting/entity/components/crafting/{craft_recipe, input_script, output_script}.rs` (`CraftRecipe.Load`/`LoadIO`/`LoadOutputMapper`/`LoadOverlayMapper`/`InitLoadPP`/`OnScriptsLoaded`/`OnPostWorldDictionaryInit`, `canBeResearched`, `canAlwaysBeResearched`, `getResearchSkillLevel`, `getHighestSkillRequirement` with its AutoLearnAll-gated-by-AutoLearnAny quirk; `InputScript.Load` and the `-`/`+` part of its OnPostWorldDictionaryInit; `OutputScript.Load`/`OnPostWorldDictionaryInit`), `zombie/entity/components/crafting/recipe/{output_mapper, overlay_mapper}.rs`, the enums `InputFlag`, `OutputFlag`, `ItemApplyMode`, `FluidMatchMode`, `ResourceType`, `zombie/scripting/objects/timed_action_script.rs` (`TimedActionScript.Load`, for the lookup), `item_tag.rs` (`ItemTag.get` over the 459 ids `tools/gen_item_tags.py` reads from `ItemTag`), `zombie/util/hash/pz_hash.rs` (`PZHash.murmur_64`), and in `ScriptManager` the craftRecipe and timedAction buckets (`CreateFromTokenPP`, `LoadScripts` with ResetExisting/RemoveLoadError, `getAllScripts` in HashMap order) and the lookups `getModule`, `ScriptBucketCollection.getScript` (fullTypeToScriptMap) and `ScriptBucket.get` (dotInName, imports) for items, craft recipes and timed actions; in `java.rs` `split("\\s+")`, `Math.round(float)`, `indexOf`/`substring` by UTF-16 index. Measured on the way: CraftRecipe and TimedActionScript keep BaseScriptObject's empty `reset`, so a later body of the same name amends the script (inputs appended); a dedicated server runs with Core.debug false, so unknown keys and blocks are only logged. Not reproduced (not compared): `iconTexture` (no textures on a server), `categoryBits` (TaggedObjectManager bits assigned in `CraftRecipeManager.Init`), the inputs' resolved `items`/`itemScriptCache` (needs the items' tags and the `getAllItems` order; for an input with several tags the original's order follows identity hashes), `fluidFilter`/`filteredFluidCache`, `energies`, the outputs' `fluid`/`energy` (`Fluid.Get`, `Energy.Get`: their not-found exceptions are not ported either), the side effects on items (`getUsedInRecipes`, `isUsedInBuildRecipes`, `setIsCraftRecipeProduct`, `addResearchableRecipe`), the `component CraftRecipe` of entity scripts (CraftRecipeComponentScript, a separate list in 183 entity files; also `getCraftRecipe`'s fallback to it), mods (`ChooseGameInfo.getModDetails`), reloading. UNKNOWN: the error paths no vanilla recipe takes (unknown types and params, bad numbers, duplicate tools or props, missing mappers, items or timed actions) are ported from the Java text but not exercised: no vanilla recipe throws (the original's log shows none). The list order of `getAllCraftRecipes` is ported but not compared (the dump is sorted); it only decides which recipes an exception would leave unprocessed.

## Animation sets and action groups by direct port (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh animsets` → `OK   animsets: 91705 lines identical`. The original runs standalone (`AnimSetsProbe`, a game case: `ZomboidFileSystem.init` over the game folder, then `ActionContext`'s class initialiser registers the condition factories). It loads every folder of `media/AnimSets` with `AnimationSet.GetAnimationSet(name, false)` and every folder of `media/actiongroups` with `ActionGroup.getActionGroup`, both in name order. Its output is compared with the port's (`pz-dump animsets`). The Java output was the same in two runs.

What is compared:
- **Animation sets.** All 28 sets and their 487 states (key, name, default index).
- **Nodes.** The 2859 selectable nodes in their selection order (`AnimState.addNode` with `compareSelectionConditions`) and the 74 abstract nodes. For each node: every field after JAXB binding and `x_extends` inheritance (1898 of the 2933 files extend another, up to `../../../GrappledDefault.xml`), the speed scale as parsed, and the blend curve (the one `LerpSequence` in the data, with its raw entries and the order its sort leaves). Also its 1243 2D blends, its conditions with parsed values and variable references, and its 4252 events (plain, `SetVariable`, `FlagWhileAlive`). Blend triangles are compared as node indices (IDREFs) and the hull of the 232 blend pickers as a sorted index list. Then the 8534 transitions with their conditions, the sub-state bone weights and the `transitionOut` node.
- **Assets.** The AnimNode asset table: 2933 files, none failed.
- **Action groups.** All 28 groups, each with its initial state and its 479 states in folder order. For each state: the grappler flag, tags (32 files) and child tags (45 files), the 8 read-only state variables (all `deferred`, Bool), and the 2710 transitions after `sortTransitions`. That covers the 4578 conditions (`CharacterVariableCondition` operators and parsed values, 314 event conditions) and both state lookups.

Ported:
- `PZXmlUtil.parseXml` with `x_include` / `x_extends` and `resolve`. The deerdead transitions include `../defaultTransitions.xml`.
- `ZomboidFileSystem` as far as these loaders use it: the file map, `resolveFileOrGUID`, `getRelativeFile`, `resolveRelativePath`, `resolveAllDirectories` / `resolveAllFiles` and `getMediaFile`.
- The JAXB binding of `AnimNode` and its parts: `Anim2DBlend`, `Anim2DBlendTriangle` (IDREF), `AnimCondition`, `AnimEvent` and its two subclasses, `AnimTransition`, `AnimBoneWeight`, `BoneAxis`, `GrappleOffsetBehaviour`, the `zombie.core.math.interpolators` classes and `InterpolatorSlot`.
- `AnimNode.Parse`, `Anim2DBlendPicker.SetPickTriangles`, `AnimState.Parse` / `addNode`, `AnimationSet.Load` and `AnimNodeAssetManager`.
- The action loaders: `ActionGroup` (`load`, `loadGroupData`, `getOrCreate`), `ActionState` (`load`, `parse`, `parseActionState`, `parseStateVariables`, `parseTags`, `sortTransitions`), `ActionTransition`, `CharacterVariableCondition` (`parseValue` as written: the int part of `-5` stays positive, a backslash in a quoted value is kept), `EventOccurred`, `EventNotOccurred` and `LuaCall`.
- `AnimationVariableSource` with the handle pool and the Bool, String and Float slots.
- `java::list_sort` (the TimSort path for fewer than 32 elements) and `java_collator::compare_english`.

The English collator was measured with `CollatorProbe`. On printable ASCII it has one element per character and no contractions, it compares at TERTIARY strength, and the results were identical on JDK 27 and the game's JRE 25. It decides the order in which an action state reads its files, so "corpseDraggedLegs-onback.xml" comes before "corpseDragged-onback.xml".

Not reproduced: the animation runtime (`AdvancedAnimator`, `AnimLayer`, `LiveAnimNode`, playback, skinning, `AnimationClip` assets and models), the `ActionContext` runtime and condition evaluation, mods, file watchers and reload, the log text (warnings for self transitions and unknown elements).

UNKNOWN:
- JAXB details:
  - how JAXB handles whitespace around enum values (the port assumes an exact match);
  - a primitive value JAXB cannot convert (the port keeps the field);
  - a node file whose root is not `animNode` (none in the data).
- The pick order of the blend-picker hull. The original keeps the hull in an identity `HashSet`, so the comparison sorts it.
- Handle-pool spellings in the running game, where other code may create a handle first with a different spelling.
- `replaceAll` with names containing `$` or `\` (an error in the port).
- `List.sort` with 32 or more elements (an error in the port).
- File names outside printable ASCII in the collator (an error in the port).
- `Enum` state variables (`AnimationVariableEnumParser`; no game file uses them).

## The game's own `media/lua/shared` + `media/lua/server` on the ported Lua VM, with the Java state they fill (2026-11-16)
`sh tools/oracle.sh luaserver` → `OK   luaserver: 271022 lines identical`. The original `LuaManager.init`, then `LoadDirBase("shared")` and `LoadDirBase("server")`, standalone (`LuaLoadEnvProbe` with `shared server`), against the port (`pz-dump luaserver`). Compared as for `luaload` (below): the order of the 668 files loaded (including the 72 `WorldGen/features` and `biomes` files `Features.lua`/`Biomes.lua` require through `WorldGenUtils.INSTANCE:getFiles`, in `Files.walk` order: depth first, each directory in the file system's listing order), every global and added key, the event callbacks; and now also the Java state the scripts fill while loading, read from the original by reflection: all 264 event names (`LuaEventManager.AddEvent` from `Vehicles.lua` adds two), every collection of a public static field the scripts changed (the six `ItemPickerJava.KeyNamer` lists, `IsoGridSquare.ignoreBlockingSprites`), the 3308 `MapObjects.OnNewWithSprite`/`OnLoadWithSprite` callbacks with their priorities, `TileOverlays` (506 tiles with room, chance, parsed usage, tiles; the overlay-to-tile map), `ContainerOverlays` (208 sprites, version 1 format), `BentFences` (100 entries with stages, collapsed and debris sprites; the sprite-to-entries map), `BrokenFences` (42 tiles, broken-left/right/all maps), the 49 `RandomizedWorldBase` clutter lists `StoryClutter_Initialization.lua` fills through `getWorld():getRandomizedWorldBase()`, `WorldGenUtils`' last file list and `SGlobalObjects`' system count (0). Hash containers are printed in key order, as their own order is not observable through the classes. Ported for it: `zombie/iso/{tile_overlays, container_overlays, bent_fences, broken_fences}.rs`, `zombie/lua/map_objects.rs`, `zombie/iso/worldgen/world_gen_utils.rs`, the exposure of 23 more classes (74 in all, 2404 method names; Perks with their ids, UIFont, the climb states, `IsoWorld`, `TextManager`, ...) and the glue in `zombie/lua/exposed_world.rs`. Measured on the way: `se.krka.kahlua.integration.expose.caller.MethodCaller.call` catches every exception of an exposed Java method, logs it and returns nothing to Lua, so a failing call is a nil result, not a Lua error (argument-count and argument-type failures still are errors); `getTextManager():getFontHeight(UIFont.Medium)` is such a case (`TextManager.font` is null before the renderer's `TextManager.Init`). The port now does the same (`exposer::Fail`). Not reproduced: `Texture.nullTextures`, the cache of failed texture lookups the scripts' `getTexture` calls fill in this renderer-less state (left out of the comparison: the port loads no textures), the sprites `BrokenFences.addBrokenTiles` adds to `IsoSpriteManager` while checking `ThumpSound`, `SGlobalObjects.registerSystem` (only called from `OnSGlobalObjectSystemInit`). UNKNOWN: the debug log text (`client`: done since, see above; the port keeps its own log of the same events: the 42 "Missing ThumpSound" lines, the `getFontHeight` NullPointerException, five "recursive require()" warnings for `WorldGen/WorldGen.lua` and one for `attachments/Priorities.lua`, which the original's `DebugType.Lua` does not print in this state).

## The game's own `media/lua/shared` on the ported Lua VM (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh luaload` → `OK   luaload: 104667 lines identical` (104722 since 2026-11-16, when the probe also prints the Java state, see above). The original `LuaManager.init` and `LuaManager.LoadDirBase("shared")` run standalone (`LuaLoadEnvProbe`: no world, no UI, no mods; the state the Lua sees is the one a bare `LuaManager.init` leaves) and so does the port (`zombie/lua/lua_manager.rs` over the ported Kahlua VM and compiler). Compared: the order in which the 374 files are loaded (`LoadDirBase`'s case-insensitive order, `require` through the search paths, `RunLua`'s loaded and loading sets; `defines.lua` and `Sandbox/Apocalypse.lua` first, because reading `SandboxOptions.instance` while exposing the API runs the SandboxOptions constructor), every global the scripts add or replace with its whole value (tables walked in their own order, numbers as raw bits, closures by file and first line, Java objects by class, enum constants by name), every key they add to a table that existed after init (`AnimalDefinitions.animals`, `math.clamp`, ...), and the callbacks of all 262 events in registration order. The values include `SandboxVars` after `SandboxOptions:initSandboxVars` (every option's `fromTable`/`toTable` with group tables, integers and enums as doubles), key bindings from `KeybindId:getId`, profession names (lower-cased resource paths), body location and trait registry objects as table keys, `Core` highlight colours (one `ColorInfo` object each), `Vector2.new()`, `copyTable` copies, and the 2 runtime errors the original itself has (`ScriptManager.instance:FindItem` is nil with no item scripts loaded). The exposed API (`zombie/lua/exposer.rs`) is generated from a measurement of the original (`sh tools/oracle.sh luaexpose` → `tools/gen_lua_expose.py` → `exposed_table.rs`): 51 classes (the 26 the shared scripts read, their nested classes and the classes of the objects they call) with their statics tables in the original's key order (static methods, enum constants, 5930 registry constants with their registration ids, Keyboard/Mouse key numbers, `new`), the names of their 1765 methods, and all 728 global functions. 15 global functions and 72 methods are written (SandboxOptions, BodyLocations/BodyLocationGroup/BodyLocation, AttachedLocations, MapSymbolDefinitions, Core, ColorInfo, Vector2, ArrayList, registry and enum constants, `getText`, `getTexture`, `copyTable`, `log`, `isClient`/`isServer`/`isSystem*`, `getDebug`); every other exposed method exists and throws "not ported" when called (PLACEHOLDER), and the other ~990 classes are absent. (Corrected 2026-11-16: an exception inside a written method was raised into Lua; the original logs it and returns nothing, see above.) The state is the standalone one: no translations loaded (`getText` returns the key), no renderer (`getTexture` is nil, map symbols are 18×18), no item scripts. Measured twice: the original's own order of the globals and of the method tables of some classes (SandboxOptions, IsoZombie, ErosionSeason, ...) changes between runs (`HashSet` and `getMethods()` order); the generated table keeps one sample, and the luaload oracle is identical with either. Not reproduced: the super-metatable chain of method tables, superclasses and interfaces of exposed classes (each has its own metatable, so lookups stop there), `Calendar`/`PZCalendar`, `Translator.getText` of a key with `%`. UNKNOWN (`shared`+`server` and `client`: above): mods, the debug log text, the `require` warnings' wording.

## Lua math, os, random and array libraries by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh fdlibm` → `OK   fdlibm: 64016 lines identical`; `sh tools/oracle.sh luaos` → `OK   luaos: 3610 lines identical`. `java.lang.FdLibm` of the game's JRE 25 (fdlibm 5.3 in Java; `StrictMath` and, on that aarch64 JRE, `Math`) is ported in `java_fdlibm.rs`: tan, asin, acos, atan, atan2, exp, expm1, log, log10, pow, sinh, cosh, tanh, sin and cos with `__ieee754_rem_pio2`/`__kernel_rem_pio2`, and `Math.scalb`. `FdLibmProbe` compares raw bits over 204000 inputs per function (special values, random bit patterns, uniform values from 1 to 1e6 and every binary scale, pow and atan2 on a grid): identical, on the JIT and with `-Xint`. Kahlua's `math.*`, the VM's `^` (`J2SEPlatform.pow`) and `IsoDirections.fromAngle` now use it. `Math.sin`/`Math.cos` are HotSpot intrinsics on that JRE and differ from `StrictMath` on about 5% of inputs (measured: 20426 and 18417 of 400000); `math.sin`/`math.cos` keep Rust's, UNKNOWN. `OsLib` (`os.date`, `os.time`, `os.difftime`) with `java.util.GregorianCalendar` in UTC (`java_calendar.rs`: Julian dates before 1582-10-15, the lenient overflow of every field, the cutover-year choice between the two calendars), `RandomLib` (`newrandom`, `r:random`, `r:seed` over `java.util.Random`, seeds from `hashCode`) and `UserdataArray` (`array.new`, `push`, `#v`, `v[i]` over a `java.util.Vector`) run `tests/lua_os_cases.txt`: 32 instants times 13 formats (`%c` included, with the default zone pinned to UTC), 3000 generated instants from 1463 to 2638, `*t` tables, `os.time` of normal, overflowing, missing and cutover dates, the errors. Kept from the original: `%I` gives 00 at noon, `%e` pads with a space and a zero, `yday` is 0 at midnight on Jan 1, `%U` counts from day 8 when Jan 1 is a Sunday, an unknown conversion prints `null`. The wall clock is the host's (`J2SEPlatform::current_time_millis`, 0 until set: PLACEHOLDER). UNKNOWN: `%c` on a machine outside UTC (it is `Date.toString` in the JVM's zone), `newrandom`'s seed (the JDK mixes in `System.nanoTime`; the port uses the seed uniquifier sequence alone), seeds from tables and functions (identity hashes), a second `ClassCastException` or `NullPointerException` from `KahluaUtil.fromDouble` in one JVM (after the first, HotSpot may throw a preallocated one with a null message, depending on the JIT). `table.newarray` (`KahluaArray`, a second table type) is still PLACEHOLDER; the game's own scripts call none of `newrandom`, `array`, `table.newarray`, and `os.date` once (the admin user list).

## Lua string library by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh luastring` → `OK   luastring: 5159 lines identical`; `sh tools/oracle.sh javachars` → `OK   javachars: 3186 lines identical`. The original `se.krka.kahlua.stdlib.StringLib` (on the game's JRE 25, through the same `LuaVmProbe` as the VM) and the port run `tests/lua_string_cases.txt`: `string.format` (Kahlua's own printf: `%d %i %u %x %X %o %c %e %E %f %g %G %s %q %%`, every flag, width and precision, negative numbers through `%x`, NaN and infinities, the `%q` quirk that leaves a backslash single, the errors), `find`/`match`/`gmatch`/`gsub` with Lua patterns (classes, sets, ranges, `%b`, `%f`, anchors, the four repetitions, position captures, captures in replacements, table and function replacements; a function result of `false` or a table is written as `null`, as the original does; pattern errors wrapped the way `findAux` and `gsub` wrap them), `split` with the literal separators the game uses (`;`, `=`, `<LINE>`, `\\.`, `\\n`, …), non-ASCII text (Cyrillic, Arabic digits, title case, `ʕ`, modifier letters), and 2500 generated patterns plus 2500 generated formats from a fixed sequence. Pattern classes `%a %l %u %d %w` use `Character.isLowerCase`/`isUpperCase`/`isDigit`, which differ from Rust's for some units (U+0295, every non-ASCII digit); the port carries the JRE's own tables, compared over all 65536 UTF-16 units by `javachars`. Found by the oracle: Kahlua's back reference check is inverted (`%1` to a closed capture is "invalid capture index", `%0` reads index -1), `loadstring` keeps only the low byte of each unit of its source, a lone surrogate is printed as `?`. UNKNOWN: a back reference to a capture slot the call never opened (the original reads the pooled `MatchState` an earlier call left; the port's slots start empty), `%f`/`%e` of numbers above about 1e300 (the original loops forever), `split` with a real regular expression (PLACEHOLDER, throws "not ported"; the game's own scripts use none).

## Lua virtual machine by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh luavm` → `OK   luavm: 1317 lines identical`. The original Kahlua VM (`J2SEPlatform.newEnvironment`, which also runs the game's `stdlib.lua`; `LuaCompiler.loadstring`; `KahluaThread.pcall`, on the game's own JRE 25) and the port run the same 49 Lua cases from `tests/lua_vm_cases.txt`, each in a fresh environment, and print everything the case prints plus what it returns (numbers as raw bits). Identical on: number to string (JDK `Double.toString`), arithmetic, `%`, `^`, string to number coercion (`"0x10"` is not a number), -0.0 turned into +0.0 by the original's boxing, comparisons, concatenation and its metamethod rules, every metamethod (`__index` chains, `__newindex`, `__call`, `__eq`, `__lt`, `__le`, `__len`, `__concat`, `__unm`), protected metatables, varargs and `select`, closures and upvalues, numeric and generic `for`, `pairs` order (insertion order), `#t` on tables with holes, -0.0 and NaN keys, `error` with any value, `pcall` results and stack trace text, the error messages of the VM and libraries, a `NullPointerException("Method name is null")` the original throws while logging an error inside an unnamed function, coroutines (create, resume, yield, status, running, wrap, dead and outside-coroutine errors), tail calls, the 1000-frame stack overflow, `loadstring` and its syntax errors, `table.*`, `math.*` without the functions below, `string.sub`/`char`/`byte`/`lower`/`upper`/`reverse`/`trim`/`len`/`rep`, `tostring`/`tonumber`/`type`/`rawget`/`rawset`/`rawequal`/`unpack`/`setmetatable`/`getmetatable`/`collectgarbage` options. Not ported (calling it throws "not ported"): `table.newarray`. The string, math, os, random and array libraries are in the sections above. UNKNOWN: `collectgarbage("count")` (zeros in the port), the error logs and `Core.debug`. The game's own `media/lua/shared` runs on this VM since E39 (first section); the window client still uses `zomboid-lua`.

## Lua compiler by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh luacompile` → `OK   luacompile: 103506 lines identical`. The original Kahlua compiler (`org.luaj.kahluafork.compiler.LexState.compile`, run on the game's own bundled JRE 25 because UTF-8 decoding and `Double.parseDouble` are JDK behaviour) and the port compile the same sources and print every prototype: name, file statics, parameters, vararg flag, max stack, upvalue count, every instruction word, line table, constants (doubles as raw bits), local names, nested prototypes; or the exception message. Identical on: all 1395 `media/lua/**/*.lua` files of the game (20407 prototypes, 1.45 million instructions; none fails), read the way `LuaManager.RunLuaInternal` reads them (UTF-8 reader, chunk name = file name), and 49 hand-written cases in `tests/lua_compile_cases.txt`: numbers (hex, exponents, `f`/`d` suffixes Kahlua accepts, malformed), long strings and comments, escapes, non-ASCII text (the lexer keeps only the low byte of each UTF-16 unit and re-decodes as UTF-8), a byte order mark (the original throws ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException while building the error message, and so does the port), every syntax error message, `continue` not being a keyword, constant folding, `and`/`or` jump lists, table constructors past 50 array items, the 200-locals, 60-upvalues, 200-syntax-levels and jump-length limits. Not ported yet: the Kahlua VM that runs these prototypes, the standard library, `Core.debug` local-variable line ranges.

## Outfit randomization by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh outfitrandom` → `OK   outfitrandom: 23310 lines identical`. Inside the booted original server, its own `LocationRNG`, `Color.HSBtoRGB`, `OutfitRNG.randomImmutableColor`, `ClothingDecals.getRandomDecal` and `ClothingItemReference.randomize` are run with fixed seeds and compared with the port's. Results match on: the generator's state and its floats and bounded ints for 7 seeds and 9 bounds (0, negative and Integer.MAX_VALUE included), the per-square seed; HSB to RGB for 192 inputs, out-of-range ones included; 80 random clothing colours with and without the NoBlackClothes sandbox option; the 21 decal groups of `clothingDecals.xml` (case-insensitive lookup, 6 seeded picks each, unknown and blank names); and every item of a clone of every outfit of `clothing.xml` (170 female and 239 male, in name order; the original shuffles them) randomized under its own seed, with and without NoBlackClothes: worn or not, hue, tint to the bit, base texture, picked sub item, texture choice and T-shirt decal, plus the next draw afterwards. UNKNOWN: a random hue (no outfit item in the data allows it), mods' decal groups, the hair, beard, top and pants part of `Outfit.Randomize`, and the decal files' contents. Zombies are not dressed from this yet.

## Game time and climate of saves by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh gametime` → `OK   gametime: 10 lines identical`. Inside the booted original server, `GameTime.load` reads the save's `map_t.bin` (world version 249, 566 bytes, read to the end) as a single-player game does (`GameServer.server` false during the call), starting with no mod data tables. The port reads the same bytes. Results match on: the clock and calendar (11:25, day 8, month 6, 1993, 0 nights, target 750, multiplier 1), `getHour`, `getMinutes`, `getWorldAgeHours`, the minute stamp (265), the game time's mod data (`dynamicRadio`: one channel table, compared as sorted keys), the poisonous berry and mushroom, the helicopter (day 7, 15 to 18), and the climate's simplex offsets, snow, fog and air front. Also matched: the weather period's running flag (not running), the three thunder clouds' flags (none running), and the climate mod table (absent). The save has no running weather period or thunder cloud. So stage and cloud loading, admin overrides (server saves), client loading, a file without the "GMTM" header, and loading into a table that already has entries are ported but UNKNOWN. The state of the two mod data tables in a real single-player start before the load is also UNKNOWN. Day/night lighting from this time is not wired yet.

## Animal zones and meta cells of saves by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh animalzones` → `OK   animalzones: 125 lines identical`. Inside the booted original server, `IsoMetaCell.load` reads the save's `metagrid/metacell_32_45.bin` and `IsoMetaGrid.loadAnimalZones` reads its `map_animals.bin` (world version 249, read to the last byte). The port does the same into a meta grid built from the same lot headers. Results match on everything compared: the cell's "animal zones generated" flag (set) and all 121 animal zones (ids, type, name, box, level, geometry, width, points, original name, action, animal type, both spawn flags, junction lists). The zones are compared in id order. All 121 are polylines: 45 Eat, 39 Follow, 34 Sleep, 3 with an empty action. The save stores every one of these zones with the box (2147483647, 2147483647) 2×2, which is how the game wrote them. The original therefore puts none of them into a cell, and neither does the port. The file has no junctions. So the cell registration of animal zones and junction loading (`distanceFromStart`) are ported but unmeasured: UNKNOWN. Animal zones made at a new world's start from `objects.lua`, their junctions, and the animal population are not ported yet.

## Zone areas and triangles by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh zonetriangles` → `OK   zonetriangles: 14556 lines identical`. On every one of the save's 5,497 zones the original server calls `Zone.getTotalArea`. For the 38 polygons and the 664 wide polylines this triangulates the zone through the native `libPZClipper`: the `Clipper` union, then mapbox earcut. The port does the same with its own Clipper2 and earcut. All 702 triangulations are bit-identical: the triangle corners, each triangle's area and the total area, 3,202 triangles in all. No zone raised an exception. The other 4,795 zones return width times height, also identical. The save's rings have at most 40 points and no holes. So earcut's z-order path (rings over 80 points) and hole bridging are ported but unmeasured: UNKNOWN. So are the native `clipAABB` and `getPolygon` (zone splitting), clip paths and offsets. `pickRandomLocation`, which draws from these triangles with the game's `Rand`, is not ported yet.

## Zones of saves by direct port (2026-11-15)
`sh tools/oracle.sh mapzone` → `OK   mapzone: 6991 lines identical`. Inside the booted original server, `IsoMetaGrid.loadZone` reads the isolated save's `map_zone.bin` (world version 249, read to the last byte). The port reads it into a meta grid built from the same lot headers. Both register the same 5,497 zones in the same order: 4,794 rectangles, 38 polygons, 664 polylines with a width and 1 without. That covers 2,703 Nav roads, 1,552 ZombiesType, 353 Ranch, 332 SpawnPoint, 171 ZoneStory, 168 Region and others. Fields, points, ids and the preferred-type flag (2,744) all match, and no zone is rejected or skipped. The 957,848 zone registrations in 817,146 meta chunks of 1,489 cells are identical. They are compared per cell as a hash over chunk index, zone count and zone indices. They exercise `Zone.intersects` on every geometry, including the outlines of the 664 wide polylines that the game's native Clipper library makes. The outline points themselves (`sh tools/oracle.sh zoneoutlines` → `OK   zoneoutlines: 7655 lines identical`, in the default run since 2026-11-15): all 664 are byte-identical to the native ones, point for point in the native order, first vertex included. They are made by the ported Clipper2 1.5.4 `ClipperOffset` offset and the `Clipper64` Vatti union (`crate::native::clipper2`). That case exercises only the closed-subject union with the positive rule into a `PolyTree64`. The engine's open paths, clip paths, the other clip types and fill rules, and the flat-path output are ported but are checked only by hand-made unit cases, not against the native library: UNKNOWN. Intersection nodes at the same point are sorted stably; libc++'s `std::sort` is not stable, so a different processing order there is possible and unmeasured. The save has no spawned-zombie zones, no construction marks and no pre-215 ids, so those paths are ported but unchecked. Zone triangulation (random points in a zone), zone stories and the client's use of zones are not ported yet.

## Rooms and buildings of saves by direct port (2026-11-14)
`sh tools/oracle.sh mapmeta` → `OK   mapmeta: 4926 lines identical`. The original server boots with the isolated save's `map_basements.bin` in its own save folder and loads its 785 basement placements, and so does the port: the same 9,978 buildings with the same ids and meta ids. Then the original `IsoMetaGrid.load` and the port read the save's `map_meta.bin` (world version 249, cells −250..250, read to the last byte) and leave the same state in all 4,065 loaded cells. That is 91,646 rooms (34 explored, 22,664 with lights on) and 9,978 buildings (662 alarmed with their alarm decay, 27 seen, 1 visited, key ids and loot respawn hours), plus 125 possible stashes. The 4 room meta ids of cell 25,33 that the original reports as invalid are logged identically by the port. The comparison runs after both sides reset rooms and buildings to their Java field defaults. What `IsoMetaGrid.Create` draws from the global `Rand` at boot (initial lights, alarms, key ids) still differs for rooms the save does not cover: UNKNOWN parity, not reproducible. The save has no safehouses, non-PvP zones, factions, designation zones or unique stories, so those readers are ported but unchecked. The client does not use this state yet (lights, alarms, explored rooms on the map).
